libname BUnica '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/comp_total';
libname HOML "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";
libname tuyra '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/tuyra';
libname simul '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/shared';
libname AUXL '/infogrc/utils/bases';
libname BASES '/riscosbvf/BA/bases';
libname DBRSOLAR '/infogrc/desdobro-solar/bases';

data tuyra.solar_rebate_0725;
set solar_rebate_0725;
run;


proc sql;

Create table base_solar as
Select distinct
a.*
,(a.DtPrimeiroVencimento - a.DtContrato)*(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrCarencia
,Case when (a.DtContrato < '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate
when (a.DtContrato >= '03jun2024'd and a.DtContrato < '01jul2024'd and e.TABELA is not null) then e.TxRebate
when (a.DtContrato >= '01jul2024'd and f.TABELA is not null) then f.TxRebate
end as TxRebate
,c.VrTac
,d.NmCampanhaCupom
,Case when (a.DtContrato < '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
when (a.DtContrato >= '03jun2024'd and a.DtContrato < '01jul2024'd and e.TABELA is not null) then e.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
when (a.DtContrato >= '01jul2024'd and f.TABELA is not null) then f.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
else a.VpTxGerencialRebate end
as VpTxNet

from HOML.tbproducao as a
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as E on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=E.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=e.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0724 as F on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=F.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=f.PRAZO)
left join HOML.tboriginacao as c on (a.NuProposta = c.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as d on (d.NuProposta = a.NuProposta)

where a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.NuAnoMes >= 202405 

order by a.NuAnoMes

;quit;

proc sql;

	create table dados_medios_solar as

		select DISTINCT
			a.NuAnoMes
			,CASE WHEN a.cdparceirocomercial in (418254) THEN 'MFS'
				ELSE 'CN' END AS Canal
			,a.TpPessoa
			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrMargemContribuicao)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcMC
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao
			,sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrFinanciamento
			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE
			,sum(a.VrTac)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VrTarifaAvaliacaoBem)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTAB
			,sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxJuros)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,sum(case when (a.VrTarifaCadastro) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as MarcTarifa
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoOriginacao
            ,sum(a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoManutencao
			,sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as CARENCIA

		from base_solar as a
		

	group by a.NuAnoMes, Canal, a.TpPessoa
	order by a.NuAnoMes, Canal, a.TpPessoa

;quit;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.RENT_SOLAR_PONTUAL AS
   SELECT t1.NuAnoMes,
          t1.NuProposta,
          t1.DtEntradaProposta,
          t1.NuContrato,
          t1.SqContratoFinanceiro,
          t1.DtContrato,
          t1.DtInicioVigencia,
          t1.DtPrimeiroVencimento,
          t1.NmGrupoProdutoMeta,
          t1.CdParceiroComercial,
          t1.NmParceiroComercial,
          t1.TpPessoa,
          t1.NuCpfCnpj,
          t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento,
          t1.QtParcelas,
          t1.NuScoreFim,
          t1.FxScoreFim,
          t1.NuRating,
          t1.VrProducao,
          t1.VrContrato,
          t1.VrEntrada,
          t1.VrFinanciamento,
          t1.VrPerdaEstimada,
          t2.VrTac,
          t1.VpTxJuros,
          t1.TxTirContabil,
          t1.VpTirContabil,
          t1.VpPrazo,
          t1.VrSeguros,
          t1.VrCarencia,
          t1.TxRebate,
          t1.VpTxNet
	FROM WORK.BASE_SOLAR AS t1
	left join HOML.tboriginacao as t2 on (t1.NuProposta = t2.NuProposta);
      QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.DADOS_MEDIO_PONTUAL AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
			t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento,

			sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as QTDContratos,
			sum(t1.VrProducao) as Prod,
			sum(t1.VrCarencia)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as Carencia,
			sum(t1.VpPrazo)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as Prazo,
			sum(t1.VrProducao)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as TM,
			sum(t1.VrSeguros)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as Seguros,
			sum(t1.VrTac)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as TAC,
			sum(t1.VpTxNet)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as TxNet,
		 	sum(t1.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(t1.VrFinanciamento) as PE,
		    sum(t1.VpTirContabil)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as TIR
FROM WORK.RENT_SOLAR_PONTUAL AS t1
where t1.CdParceiroComercial = 402424 
group by t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento;
	QUIT;

	%include '/infogrc/utils/IQ.sas';

%CRIAVARDB;
PROC SQL;
%CONECTADB(server=CTR);
CREATE TABLE Tabelas_DELTA AS SELECT * FROM CONNECTION TO DbCon(
SELECT
CdParametroVariacaoTabelaFnnco as Delta,
NuCpfInicioFaixaTaxaJuro,
NuCpfFimFaixaTaxaJuro,
NuParcelaInicioFaixa,
NuParcelaFimFaixa,
AaVeiculoInicioFaixa,
AaVeiculoFimFaixa,
PcProbabilidadeAprovacaoInicia,
PcProbabilidadeAprovacaoFinal,
VrVariacaoTabelaFinanciamento,
FlAtivo
FROM DBCOR.dbo.TbFaixaParametroVariacao
WHERE FlAtivo = 'S'
and CdParametroVariacaoTabelaFnnco in (8,28, 48)
)
;
RUN;

/* TASK: TOTAIS */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 002 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 91 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: TOTAIS */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-b9sy1jJSw9UzRyaO/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
libname BUnica '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/comp_total';
libname HOML "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";
libname tuyra '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/tuyra';
libname tomaz '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/tomaz';

data tuyra.saude_rebate_1023;
set saude_rebate_1023;
run;

proc sql;

Create table base_total_txnet as
Select distinct
a.*
,case when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar', 'FGTS') then e.VrTac else a.VrTarifaCadastro
end as VrTacOrigin

,Case when (a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Saude') and b.tabela is not null and a.DtContrato < '17jul2023'd) then b.Tx_Net*a.VrProducao
when (a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Saude') and c.tabela is not null and a.DtContrato < '03oct2023'd) then c.Tx_Net*a.VrProducao
when (a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Saude') and d.tabela is not null and a.DtContrato >= '03oct2023'd) then d.Tx_Net*a.VrProducao
when (a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and sol.TABELA is not null) then sol.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
else a.VpTxGerencialRebate end as VpTxNet

,case when (a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('FGTS') and f.Tabela is not null) then ((F.Comissao_Total/100)*a.VrProducao)
else a.VrComissaoTotal end as VrComissaoTotal_Correto

from HOML.tbproducao as a
left join tuyra.saude_rebate_0723 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.tabela and A.QtParcelas=b.Prazo)
left join tuyra.saude_rebate_0723_II as C on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=C.tabela and A.QtParcelas=c.Prazo)
left join tuyra.saude_rebate_1023 as D on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=D.tabela and A.QtParcelas=d.Prazo)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0224 as sol on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=sol.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=sol.PRAZO)
left join HOML.tboriginacao E on (a.NuProposta = E.NuProposta)
left join tuyra.fgts_comissao_1023 F on (a.CdTabelaFinanciamento = F.Tabela)
where a.NuAnoMes >= 202312

order by a.NuAnoMes
;quit;

proc sql;

Create table base_total as
Select distinct
a.*
,(a.DtPrimeiroVencimento - a.DtContrato)*(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrCarencia
,case
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CVG Leves', 'CVG Pesados') and a.DsTipoCanalProducao in ('MULTIMARCAS','CONCESSIONARIAS') and a.NmParceiroComercial not in ('QUERO FINANCIAR', 'FINANZERO BRASIL', 'FINANZERO BRASIL CP SANTANA', 'CP GARAN FINANZERO', 'QUERO FINANCIAR CP  SANTANA', 'AME DIGITAL BRASIL INSTITUICAO DE PAGAME', 'CREDMISSOES', 'ECOM COMUNICACOES LTDA', 'CREDTRUCK','EAI CLUBE AUTOMOBILISTA S.A.', 'EASYC S.A.', 'GRINGO O MELHOR AMIGO DO MOTORISTA LTDA.', 'JUROS BAIXOS JA','S3 TECNOLOGIA E SOLUCOES DE PAGAMENTOS S','CP GARAN CVG', 'CP GARAN CVG SIMULADOR SITE', 'CP GARANT CVG II') and  a.CdParceiroComercial not in(449109,450421,452671,455884,460298,448756,433956,454428,451257) then 'CV'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CVG Leves', 'CVG Pesados') and a.NmParceiroComercial in ('QUERO FINANCIAR', 'FINANZERO BRASIL', 'FINANZERO BRASIL CP SANTANA', 'CP GARAN FINANZERO', 'QUERO FINANCIAR CP  SANTANA', 'AME DIGITAL BRASIL INSTITUICAO DE PAGAME', 'CREDMISSOES', 'ECOM COMUNICACOES LTDA', 'CREDTRUCK','EAI CLUBE AUTOMOBILISTA S.A.', 'EASYC S.A.', 'GRINGO O MELHOR AMIGO DO MOTORISTA LTDA.', 'JUROS BAIXOS JA','S3 TECNOLOGIA E SOLUCOES DE PAGAMENTOS S') or a.CdParceiroComercial in(449109,450421,452671,455884,460298,448756,433956,454428,451257) then 'Digital Parcerias'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CVG Leves', 'CVG Pesados') and a.NmParceiroComercial in ('CP GARAN CVG', 'CP GARAN CVG SIMULADOR SITE', 'CP GARANT CVG II') then 'Digital Interno'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.cdparceirocomercial in (418254) then 'MFS'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') and a.CdParceiroComercial = 390382 then 'Canais Internos'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') and a.CdParceiroComercial = 402395 then 'CP Neon'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') and a.CdParceiroComercial = 431392 then 'Banco Digital'
	when a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') and a.CdParceiroComercial in (360064, 442340) then 'Telemarketing'
	else 'CN'
End as AgrupamentoCanal

from base_total_txnet as a

where a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Privado', 'Solar','CVG Leves', 'CVG Pesados','CP','FGTS','Saude')

order by a.NuAnoMes

;quit;

proc sql;

	create table dados_medios_total as

		select DISTINCT
			a.NuAnoMes
			,CASE WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CVG Leves', 'CVG Pesados' ) THEN '1.CVG'
				WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Privado') THEN '2.Privado'
				WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') THEN '3.Solar'
				WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') THEN '4.CP'
				WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('FGTS') THEN '5.FGTS'
                WHEN a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Saude') THEN '6.Saude' END AS PRODUTO
			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrMargemContribuicao)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcMC
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal_Correto+VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao
			,sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrFinanciamento
			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE
			,sum(a.VrTacOrigin)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VrTarifaAvaliacaoBem)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTAB
			,sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxJuros)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,sum(case when (a.VrTarifaCadastro) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as MarcTarifa
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal_Correto)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoOriginacao
            ,sum(a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoManutencao
			,sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as CARENCIA

		from base_total as a

	group by 1,2
	order by a.NuAnoMes, PRODUTO

;quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: TOTAIS */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 002 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: CANAIS_CP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 003 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 91 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: CANAIS_CP */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-iFFQl8ZF3A8jQvl7/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
libname BUnica '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/comp_total';
libname HOML "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";
libname tuyra '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/tuyra';





data tbproducaoCP;
	set HOML.tbproducao;

		CARENCIA = DtPrimeiroVencimento - DtContrato;
		VrCarencia = VrFinanciamento*CARENCIA;

		where nmgrupoprodutometa in ('CP') and NuAnoMes >= 202312 and CdTabelaFinanciamento not in ("BVUJZ", "BV2DC");

		FORMAT PARCEIRO $CHAR13.;

		if CdParceiroComercial = 373499 then PARCEIRO='FINANZERO';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 406874 then PARCEIRO='FINANZERO';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 393446 then PARCEIRO='ECRED';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 391334 then PARCEIRO='BPC';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 399597 then PARCEIRO='Easy Credito';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 408614 then PARCEIRO='Juros Baixos';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 378436 then PARCEIRO='Guia Bolso';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 390382 then PARCEIRO='Minha BV';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 407516 then PARCEIRO='Just';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 402395 then PARCEIRO='CP Neon';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial in (360064, 442340) then PARCEIRO='Telemarketing';
			ELSE IF CdParceiroComercial = 431392 then PARCEIRO='Banco Digital';
		else PARCEIRO='CN';

run;


proc sql;

	create table BASE_CP_COMPLETA as

		select DISTINCT
			A.*
			,B.PE_Credito
			,G.PE_Credito as PE_Credito_Teste
			,B.POLÍTICA
			,CASE WHEN A.NuAnoMes<=202201 THEN B.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes=202202 THEN C.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202203,202204,202205) THEN D.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202206,202207) THEN E.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202208,202209) THEN F.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202210,202301) THEN G.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202302,202304) THEN H.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
			 WHEN A.NuAnoMes in (202305,202306) THEN I.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento
		     ELSE J.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento END AS VrPECredito

	from tbproducaoCP as A
			left join tuyra.tabela_CP as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN b.PRAZO_I AND b.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=B.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.tabela_CP_atu as C on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=C.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN c.PRAZO_I AND c.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=C.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0322 as D on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=D.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN D.PRAZO_I AND D.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=D.PARCERIA)
            left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0622 as E on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=E.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN E.PRAZO_I AND E.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=E.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0822 as F on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=F.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN F.PRAZO_I AND F.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=F.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_1022 as G on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=G.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN G.PRAZO_I AND G.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=G.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0223 as H on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=H.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN H.PRAZO_I AND H.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=H.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0523 as I on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=I.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN I.PRAZO_I AND I.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=I.PARCERIA)
			left join tuyra.TABELA_CP_0723 as J on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=J.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN J.PRAZO_I AND J.PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=J.PARCERIA)
	order by A.NuAnoMes,A.PARCEIRO

;quit;














proc sql;

	create table dados_medios_cp as

		select DISTINCT
			a.NuAnoMes
			,a.PARCEIRO
			,sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as CARENCIA
			,sum(a.VrPECredito)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as PE_Credito
			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrMargemContribuicao)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcMC
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao
			,sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrFinanciamento
			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE
			,sum(a.VrTarifaCadastro)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VrTarifaAvaliacaoBem)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTAB
			,sum(a.VpTxGerencialRebate)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxJuros)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,sum(case when (a.VrTarifaCadastro) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as MarcTarifa

	from BASE_CP_COMPLETA as a

	group by 1,2
	order by a.NuAnoMes, PARCEIRO

;quit;




proc sql;

	create table BASE_CP_PROJ as

		select DISTINCT
			A.*
			,B.PE_Credito
			,B.TAXA
			,(A.VrProducao*B.TAXA) AS VpTxPac
			,B.PE_Credito*A.VrFinanciamento AS VrPECredito

	from tbproducaoCP as A
			inner join tuyra.PROJECAO_CP_0723 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and (A.QtParcelas BETWEEN PRAZO_I AND PRAZO_F)and A.PARCEIRO=B.PARCERIA)

	order by A.NuAnoMes,A.PARCEIRO

;quit;

proc sql;

	create table dados_medios_proj as

		select DISTINCT
			a.NuAnoMes
			,a.PARCEIRO
			,sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as CARENCIA
			,sum(a.VrPECredito)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as PE_Credito
			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrMargemContribuicao)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcMC
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao
			,sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrFinanciamento
			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE
			,sum(a.VrTarifaCadastro)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VrTarifaAvaliacaoBem)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTAB
			,sum(a.VpTxGerencialRebate)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxPac)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,sum(case when (a.VrTarifaCadastro) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as MarcTarifa

	from BASE_CP_PROJ as a

	group by 1,2
	order by a.NuAnoMes, PARCEIRO

;quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: CANAIS_CP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 003 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Cálculo spread */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 004 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 91 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Cálculo spread */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-Q7kh2Pbiwiaepwbf/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
libname HOML "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";
libname REBATE '/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/tuyra';





proc sql;

Create table base_spread as
Select distinct
a.NuAnoMes
,a.DsLoginN1C
,a.CdFilial
,Case when
b.TABELA is not null then b.TxRebate*a.VrProducao else a.VpTxGerencialRebate end
as VpTxNet
,a.VrProducao
,a.VpTirContabil
from HOML.tbproducao as a
left join REBATE.solar_rebate_1123 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
where a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.NuAnoMes = 202310 and a.CdFilial in (347, 348, 349, 350, 351, 352, 353)

;quit;

proc sql;
Create table base_spread_filial as
Select distinct
a.CdFilial
,(1+(sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao)))/(1+(sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao)))-1 as Spread
from base_spread a
group by a.CdFilial
;quit;

proc sql;
Create table base_spread_GR as
Select distinct
a.DsLoginN1C
,(1+(sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao)))/(1+(sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao)))-1 as Spread
from base_spread a
group by a.DsLoginN1C
;quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Cálculo spread */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 004 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: INAD_SOLAR */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 005 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 91 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: INAD_SOLAR */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-g3D1bFPyWU6mGRmd/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
libname shared2 "/riscosbvf/plan_mesas/comp_total";

PROC SQL;
    CREATE TABLE PRODUCAO_SOLAR AS
        SELECT *,
                CASE WHEN Inad30_P1 = 1 THEN VrParcela ELSE 0 END AS Inad_30_1pmt,
                CASE WHEN ElegInad30_P1 = 1 THEN VrParcela ELSE 0 END AS Val_prt_30_1pmt,
                case
                    when CdLoja in (418254	,
432534	,
434102	,
443365	,
444897	,
445782	,
445788	,
445931	,
446035	,
446870	,
446881	,
449611	,
450503	,
451140	,
451676	,
451692	,

LIBNAME Homolog2 "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PROD_PARCEIROS_SOLAR AS
   SELECT
		t1.DtEntradaProposta,
		t2.DtContrato,
		intnx('month',t1.DtEntradaProposta,0,"BEGINNING") FORMAT=DATE7. as DataRef_Orig,
		intnx('month',t2.DtContrato,0,"BEGINNING") FORMAT=DATE7. as DataRef_Contrato,
		t1.NuProposta,
		t2.NuContrato,
		t1.StProposta,

	          t1.CdParceiroComercial,
	          t1.NmParceiroComercial,
	          t1.NuCnpjLojaFavorecido,
			  t1.NmParceiroComercialFavorecido,
			  t1.DsFilial,

		 
		  Case when
			t1.cdparceirocomercial = 418254  or t1.cdparceirocomercial = 378215 then "MFS"
		  Else "CS" end as CanalComercial,

            t2.VrProducao,
		    t1.VrPropostaOriginada,
	        t1.VrPropostaAprovada

      FROM HOMOLOG2.TBORIGINACAO t1
      left JOIN HOMOLOG2.TBPRODUCAO t2 ON (t1.NuProposta = t2.NuProposta)

      WHERE t1.NmGrupoProdutoMeta = 'Solar' and t1.NuProposta = 405125920
	  


	 ;

QUIT;

libname port '/portfoliomis/concessao';

DATA TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR;
set TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR_GCON TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR_FLEX;

format REFMAIS1 date9.;
REFMAIS1=intnx('month',DtProcessamento,1,'s');

format REFMAIS1_ ddmmyy10.;
       REFMAIS1_=REFMAIS1;

SAFRAREFMAIS1 = compress(put(year (REFMAIS1_) * 100 + month(REFMAIS1_), 6.))*1;
REF = compress(put(year (DtProcessamento) * 100 + month(DtProcessamento), 6.))*1;
REF_ = compress(put(year (DtProcessamento) * 100 + month(DtProcessamento), 6.));

run;

proc sql;
CREATE TABLE TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR2 as
select a.*,
b.QtDiasCarencia,
b.DtEntradaProposta,
b.CdLoja,
b.Tipo_Pes,
b.Safra_Proposta_n

from TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR a
left join PORT.BU_NN (where=(NuContrato is not missing and SubProduto = "EQUIPAMENTO FOTOVOLTAICO")) b on
a.NuContrato = b.NuContrato;
quit;

DATA TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR3;
set TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR2;

dt_final = intnx ('day',DtContrato,QtDiasCarencia);
Mob_DtContrato=intck('month',dt_final,DtProcessamento);

format dt_final2 ddmmyy10.;
       dt_final2=dt_final;

days_diff = intck('day', dt_final2, DtProcessamento);

IF days_diff > 30 then eleg_over30_proc = 1;
else eleg_over30_proc = 0;

IF days_diff > 60 then eleg_over60_proc = 1;
else eleg_over60_proc = 0;

IF days_diff > 90 then eleg_over90_proc = 1;
else eleg_over90_proc = 0;

format elegivel $40.;
IF eleg_over30_proc = 1 and eleg_over60_proc = 0 and eleg_over90_proc = 0 then elegivel = "01. Over30";
else if eleg_over30_proc = 1 and eleg_over60_proc = 1 and eleg_over90_proc = 0 then elegivel = "02. Over30/Over60";
else if eleg_over30_proc = 1 and eleg_over60_proc = 1 and eleg_over90_proc = 1 then elegivel = "03. Over30/Over60/Over90";

if eleg_over30_proc = 1 then ELEG_OVER30 = Saldo_Total;
ELSE ELEG_OVER30 = 0;

if eleg_over60_proc = 1 then ELEG_OVER60 = Saldo_Total;
ELSE ELEG_OVER60 = 0;

if eleg_over90_proc = 1 then ELEG_OVER90 = Saldo_Total;
ELSE ELEG_OVER90 = 0;

RUN;

proc sql;
create table PD_SOLAR as select
Nucontrato,
year(DtContrato)*100+month(DtContrato) as safra,
Tipo_Pes,
Safra_Proposta_n,

max(case when QtDiasAtraso >= 30 and Mob_DtContrato le  3 then 1 else 0 end) as PD30m3,
max(case when QtDiasAtraso >= 60 and Mob_DtContrato le  6 then 1 else 0 end) as PD60m6,
max(case when QtDiasAtraso >= 90 and Mob_DtContrato le  12 then 1 else 0 end) as PD90m12

from TBCARTEIRA_SOLAR3
GROUP BY 1,2,3,4;
quit;

PROC SQL;
    CREATE TABLE Cubo_PD AS
        SELECT  safra,
                Safra_Proposta_n,
                Tipo_Pes,
                sum(PD30m3) as PD30m3,
                sum(PD60m6) as PD60m6,
                sum(PD90m12) as PD90m12 ,
                count(Nucontrato) as qtde
    FROM PD_SOLAR
    group by 1,2,3
    ;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_TBORIGINACAO_0000 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT 
                     (COUNT(DISTINCT(t1.NuProposta))) AS COUNT_DISTINCT_of_NuProposta,
          
            (SUM(t1.VrTac)) FORMAT=20.2 AS SUM_of_VrTac,
          
            ((SUM(t1.VrTac))/(COUNT(DISTINCT(t1.NuProposta)))) AS TAC
      FROM WORK.QUERY_FOR_TBORIGINACAO t1;
QUIT;

proc sql;

Create table base_solar_Ecopower as
Select distinct
a.*
,(a.DtPrimeiroVencimento - a.DtContrato)*(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrCarencia
,b.TxRebate
,c.VrTac
,d.NmCampanhaCupom
,Case when b.TABELA is not null then b.TxRebate*a.VrProducao else a.VpTxGerencialRebate end
as VpTxNet


from HOML.tbproducao as a
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1123 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join HOML.tboriginacao as c on (a.NuProposta = c.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as d on (d.NuProposta = a.NuProposta)

where a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.DtContrato >= '01oct2023'd and a.CdParceiroComercial in (402424)

order by a.DtContrato

;quit;

proc sql;

Create table base_solar as
Select distinct
a.*
,(a.DtPrimeiroVencimento - a.DtContrato)*(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrCarencia
,Case when (a.DtContrato < '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate
when (a.DtContrato >= '03jun2024'd and a.DtContrato < '01jul2024'd and e.TABELA is not null) then e.TxRebate
when (a.DtContrato >= '01jul2024'd and f.TABELA is not null) then f.TxRebate
end as TxRebate
,c.VrTac
,d.NmCampanhaCupom
,Case when (a.DtContrato < '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
when (a.DtContrato >= '03jun2024'd and a.DtContrato < '01jul2024'd and e.TABELA is not null) then e.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
when (a.DtContrato >= '01jul2024'd and f.TABELA is not null) then f.TxRebate*a.VrProducao
else a.VpTxGerencialRebate end
as VpTxNet

from HOML.tbproducao as a
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as B on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=B.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as E on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=E.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=e.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0724 as F on (A.CdTabelaFinanciamento=F.TABELA and A.QtParcelas=f.PRAZO)
left join HOML.tboriginacao as c on (a.NuProposta = c.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as d on (d.NuProposta = a.NuProposta)

where a.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.NuAnoMes >= 202405 

order by a.NuAnoMes

;quit;

proc sql;

	create table dados_medios_solar as

		select DISTINCT
			a.NuAnoMes
			,CASE WHEN a.cdparceirocomercial in (418254) THEN 'MFS'
				ELSE 'CN' END AS Canal
			,a.TpPessoa
			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrMargemContribuicao)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcMC
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao
			,sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as VrFinanciamento
			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE
			,sum(a.VrTac)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VrTarifaAvaliacaoBem)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTAB
			,sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxJuros)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,sum(case when (a.VrTarifaCadastro) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as MarcTarifa
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoOriginacao
            ,sum(a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissaoManutencao
			,sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as CARENCIA

		from base_solar as a
		

	group by a.NuAnoMes, Canal, a.TpPessoa
	order by a.NuAnoMes, Canal, a.TpPessoa

;quit;

          t2.VrTac,

          t1.TxTirContabil,
          t1.VpTirContabil,

          t1.VpTxNet
	FROM WORK.BASE_SOLAR AS t1

      QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.DADOS_MEDIO_PONTUAL AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
			t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento,

			sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as QTDContratos,
			sum(t1.VrProducao) as Prod,
			sum(t1.VrCarencia)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as Carencia,
			sum(t1.VpPrazo)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as Prazo,
			sum(t1.VrProducao)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as TM,
			sum(t1.VrSeguros)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as Seguros,
			sum(t1.VrTac)/sum(t1.SqContratoFinanceiro) as TAC,
			sum(t1.VpTxNet)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as TxNet,
		 	sum(t1.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(t1.VrFinanciamento) as PE,
		    sum(t1.VpTirContabil)/sum(t1.VrProducao) as TIR
FROM WORK.RENT_SOLAR_PONTUAL AS t1
where t1.CdParceiroComercial = 402424 
group by t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento;
	QUIT;

DATA WORK.solar_rebate_1024;
    LENGTH
        PARCEIRO         $ 39
        TABELA           $ 5
        PRAZO              8
        TxRebate           8 ;
    FORMAT
        PARCEIRO         $CHAR39.
        TABELA           $CHAR5.
        PRAZO            BEST12.
        TxRebate         BEST12. ;
    INFORMAT
        PARCEIRO         $CHAR39.
        TABELA           $CHAR5.
        PRAZO            BEST12.
        TxRebate         BEST12. ;
    INFILE '/saswork/SAS_work96270022D655_srv-sasprd03n1/#LN00011'
        LRECL=67
        ENCODING="LATIN1"
        TERMSTR=CRLF
        DLM='7F'x
        MISSOVER
        DSD ;
    INPUT
        PARCEIRO         : $CHAR39.
        TABELA           : $CHAR5.
        PRAZO            : BEST32.
        TxRebate         : BEST32. ;
RUN;

libname HOML "/projetos/MISV/data/bases";

proc sql;

	Create table base_mfs as
		Select distinct
			A.NuCpfCnpj
			,A.NuAnoMes
			,A.NuProposta
			,A.VrPropostaOriginada as VrContrato
			,A.CdTabelaFinanciamento as tabela
			,A.StProposta
			,A.DtEntradaProposta
			,A.DtStatusProposta as DtPagamentoProposta
			,case when a.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGQ','BVTU0','BVX07','BVXHH','BVXgq','BVXVD','BVU2J','BVU2K','BVU2L') then "1.PADRÃO"
				when a.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXHA','BVyb3','BVYB3') then "2.COMBATE1"
				when a.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXHE','bvyb4','BVXhe','BVYB4','BVyb4') then "3.COMBATE2"
				when a.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV13J','bv13j','BV13j') then "5.ESPECIAL"
				else "4.EXCEÇÃO" end as tipo_tabela

			from HOML.tboriginacao as a

	where nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and a.cdparceirocomercial in (418254) and a.NuAnoMes >= 202312 and A.StProposta in ('A','AP','PA')

;quit;

proc sql;

	Create table resumo as
		Select distinct
		A.NuAnoMes
		,A.tipo_tabela
		,A.StProposta

		,Sum(A.VrContrato) as Total_produção

		from base_mfs as a

		group by 1,2,3
		;quit;

		*login: PORTALSOLAR_PRD
		senha: 2tetHUbr*

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_TBINFOCUPOM AS
   SELECT t1.CdParametroCampanhaCupom,
          t1.NmParametroCampanhaCupom,
          t1.CdSubProduto,
          t1.DtInicioVigencia,
          t1.DtFimVigencia,
          t1.CdCampanhaCupom,
          t1.NmCampanhaCupom,
          t1.QtMaximaUtilizacaoCupom,
          t1.QtCupomUtilizado,
          t1.NuProposta,
          t1.DtEntradaProposta,
          t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento,
          t1.txFinanciamento,
          t1.vrProposta
      FROM AUXL.TBINFOCUPOM t1
      WHERE t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '1Mar2024:0:0:0'dt;
QUIT;

DATA WORK.SAUDE_REBATE_1023;
    LENGTH
        Prazo              8
        tabela           $ 5
        Tx_Pactuada        8
        Tx_Net             8 ;
    FORMAT
        Prazo            BEST12.
        tabela           $CHAR5.
        Tx_Pactuada      BEST12.
        Tx_Net           BEST12. ;
    INFORMAT
        Prazo            BEST12.
        tabela           $CHAR5.
